SCHEMA:DB_ADD: users table. Add column. Backward compatible. Migration strategy: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS. Rollback: DROP COLUMN.

CONTRACT:ROUTE_ADD: /api/v1/users. Adding new endpoint with POST method. Breaking: No (additive). Deprecation strategy: none needed.

CONTRACT:ROUTE_REMOVE: /api/v1/orders. Removing deprecated endpoint. Breaking: Yes — all consumers affected. Deprecation strategy: migration guide + redirect.

CONTRACT:RESPONSE_ADD: /api/v1/products. Adding response field to existing endpoint. Breaking: No (additive). Deprecation strategy: announce + add before remove.

CONTRACT:RESPONSE_REMOVE: /api/v1/payments. Removing response field. Breaking: Yes — consumers parsing response. Deprecation strategy: announce + grace period.

CONTRACT:PARAM_ADD_REQ: /api/v1/subscriptions. Adding required query parameter. Breaking: Yes — all current requests will fail. Deprecation strategy: announce + dual-support.

CONTRACT:PARAM_REMOVE: /api/v1/notifications. Removing optional parameter. Breaking: Yes — consumers sending this param. Deprecation strategy: announce + remove after N months.

CONTRACT:STATUS_CODE: /api/v1/search. Changing success status code. Breaking: Yes — client status code handling. Deprecation strategy: announce + test client compatibility.

CONTRACT:HEADER: /api/v1/reports. Adding custom response header. Breaking: No (additive). Deprecation strategy: announce + add.

INFRA:DOCKER: nginx. Updating Dockerfile base image and dependencies. Risk: HIGH. State rollback: docker compose down + up.

INFRA:K8S: postgres. Modifying Kubernetes deployment manifest with resource limits. Risk: HIGH. State rollback: kubectl rollout undo.

INFRA:TERRAFORM: redis. Changing Terraform module for infrastructure provisioning. Risk: HIGH. State rollback: terraform plan revert.

INFRA:NETWORK: app-service. Updating network security group rules. Risk: CRITICAL. State rollback: restore previous security group.

INFRA:SCALING: worker. Adjusting horizontal pod autoscaler thresholds. Risk: MEDIUM. State rollback: revert HPA config.

INFRA:STORAGE: cron-job. Resizing persistent volume claim. Risk: HIGH. State rollback: revert PVC + restore data.

INFRA:IAM: message-queue. Modifying IAM role permissions. Risk: CRITICAL. State rollback: revert IAM policy.

INFRA:MONITORING: cache-cluster. Adding monitoring alert and dashboard. Risk: MEDIUM. State rollback: disable alert.

INFRA:CI: search-index. Updating CI pipeline build steps. Risk: MEDIUM. State rollback: revert CI config.

INFRA:CD: cdn-distribution. Modifying CD pipeline deployment strategy. Risk: HIGH. State rollback: revert CD config.

INCIDENT:OUTAGE: auth service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: immediate triage, assess blast radius, mitigate. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:DATA_LOSS: payment service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: data restore from backup, verify integrity. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:SECURITY: checkout service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: block attack vector, rotate credentials, audit logs. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:DEGRADED: inventory service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: identify degradation cause, implement mitigation, monitor. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:ROLLBACK: notification service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: execute rollback plan, verify pre-rollback state. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:MITIGATION: user-profile service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: apply fix, verify resolution, monitor for recurrence. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:PERFORMANCE: admin-dashboard service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: analyze bottleneck, implement optimization, validate. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:CAPACITY: search service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: scale resources, implement rate limiting, review capacity plan. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:DEPLOYMENT_FAILURE: recommendation service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: revert deployment, diagnose failure, fix, redeploy. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:COMPLIANCE: reporting service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: assess compliance gap, document, remediate. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:OUTAGE: analytics service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: immediate triage, assess blast radius, mitigate. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:DATA_LOSS: onboarding service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: data restore from backup, verify integrity. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:SECURITY: subscription service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: block attack vector, rotate credentials, audit logs. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:DEGRADED: billing service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: identify degradation cause, implement mitigation, monitor. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:ROLLBACK: shipping service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: execute rollback plan, verify pre-rollback state. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:MITIGATION: review service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: apply fix, verify resolution, monitor for recurrence. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:PERFORMANCE: rating service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: analyze bottleneck, implement optimization, validate. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

INCIDENT:CAPACITY: comment service. Severity: CRITICAL. All incidents are CRITICAL by default. Response: scale resources, implement rate limiting, review capacity plan. Post-incident: root cause analysis, monitoring gap review, prevention plan.

Cross-check: FEATURE vs REFACTOR (variant 1). FEATURE adds new behavior; REFACTOR preserves existing behavior unchanged. Key test: does the output change for the same input?. Apply this distinction when the change type is ambiguous. Conservative path: classify as the higher-risk type.

Cross-check: FEATURE vs REFACTOR (variant 2). FEATURE adds new behavior; REFACTOR preserves existing behavior unchanged. Key test: does the output change for the same input?. Apply this distinction when the change type is ambiguous. Conservative path: classify as the higher-risk type.

Cross-check: SCHEMA vs CONFIG (variant 1). SCHEMA changes data structure (tables, columns, types); CONFIG changes runtime settings (values, flags, timeouts). Key test: does the change affect persisted data shape?. Apply this distinction when the change type is ambiguous. Conservative path: classify as the higher-risk type.

The user reports a bug in the auth module but the stack trace points to a shared utility. Classify as FIX (conservative — treat runtime errors as bugs, even if the root cause is in a different module than the symptom).

The user asks to "clean up" the payment module. This is REFACTOR unless behavior changes. Verify: run existing tests before and after. If tests pass and output is identical, it is REFACTOR. If behavior changes, reclassify as FIX or FEATURE.

The user adds a field to the checkout configuration. This is CONFIG if the field changes runtime behavior. It is SCHEMA if the field changes the shape of persisted data. Check: does this field get stored in a database?

The user updates the API documentation for inventory. This is DOCS if only the documentation changes. It is CONTRACT if the documentation reveals an undocumented current behavior that consumers depend on.

The user experiments with a new deployment model in the notification module. Classify as EXPERIMENT. Duration limit: 1 month. Expiry tracking required.

The user asks "explain how user-profile works." This is ANALYSIS. No code changes. Output is explanatory. If the analysis reveals a bug, classify the explanation as ANALYSIS and the subsequent fix as FIX.

The user creates a plan for rewriting the admin-dashboard module. Classify as PLAN. Output: architecture decision record, migration plan, risk assessment. Subsequent implementation steps are FEATURE or REFACTOR.

The user adds a metrics endpoint to search. This is FEATURE (new functionality). Even though it is small, it is additive behavior that changes the API surface.

The user updates the API documentation for search. This is DOCS if only the documentation changes. It is CONTRACT if the documentation reveals an undocumented current behavior that consumers depend on.

The user experiments with a new API pattern in the recommendation module. Classify as EXPERIMENT. Duration limit: 1 month. Expiry tracking required.

The user asks "explain how reporting works." This is ANALYSIS. No code changes. Output is explanatory. If the analysis reveals a bug, classify the explanation as ANALYSIS and the subsequent fix as FIX.

The user creates a plan for rewriting the analytics module. Classify as PLAN. Output: architecture decision record, migration plan, risk assessment. Subsequent implementation steps are FEATURE or REFACTOR.

The user adds a debug endpoint to onboarding. This is FEATURE (new functionality). Even though it is small, it is additive behavior that changes the API surface.

The user reports a bug in the subscription module but the stack trace points to a shared utility. Classify as FIX (conservative — treat runtime errors as bugs, even if the root cause is in a different module than the symptom).

The user asks to "clean up" the billing module. This is REFACTOR unless behavior changes. Verify: run existing tests before and after. If tests pass and output is identical, it is REFACTOR. If behavior changes, reclassify as FIX or FEATURE.

The user adds a field to the shipping configuration. This is CONFIG if the field changes runtime behavior. It is SCHEMA if the field changes the shape of persisted data. Check: does this field get stored in a database?

The user updates the API documentation for review. This is DOCS if only the documentation changes. It is CONTRACT if the documentation reveals an undocumented current behavior that consumers depend on.

The user experiments with a new database technology in the rating module. Classify as EXPERIMENT. Duration limit: 1 month. Expiry tracking required.

The user asks "explain how comment works." This is ANALYSIS. No code changes. Output is explanatory. If the analysis reveals a bug, classify the explanation as ANALYSIS and the subsequent fix as FIX.

The user creates a plan for rewriting the social-feed module. Classify as PLAN. Output: architecture decision record, migration plan, risk assessment. Subsequent implementation steps are FEATURE or REFACTOR.
